Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Configure each runtime using the environment variables below.\n\n## 1. API Service (`api_image` on ECS Fargate)\n\n- **Binary**: `/usr/local/bin/clearledger-api`\n- **Listening port**: `8080` (`PORT=8080`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `PORT`: `\"8080\"`\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: control-plane endpoint reachable from the container (`http://aws:4566`)\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: Valkey connection URL `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`: `\"90\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"90\"`)\n  - `AUTH_ISSUER`: Cognito issuer URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_AUDIENCES`: comma-separated Cognito client IDs (`<read_client_id>,<write_client_id>,<admin_client_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_JWKS_URL`: Cognito JWKS URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>/.well-known/jwks.json`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the API CloudWatch Log Group\n  - `SERVICE_INSTANCE_ID` (optional): instance identifier returned in `X-ClearLedger-Instance` (defaults to `HOSTNAME`)\n\nUpon startup, `clearledger-api` connects to PostgreSQL and serves HTTP traffic on `0.0.0.0:8080`. The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its three indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store & Control-Plane Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane (security groups, IAM role policies, SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge schedules, CloudWatch Log Groups, Lambda environment variables, and PostgreSQL schema/constraints/triggers/indexes) and all derived data stores (SQS outbox relay, DynamoDB projections, Valkey cache entries, and the versioned S3 `ledger-audit/` archive) against PostgreSQL as specified in `services/rds.md`, `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, `services/s3.md`, `services/iam.md`, and `services/vpc.md` before exiting `0`.\n- **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n  - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including customer-managed KMS keys scheduled for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n- **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n\n\n# ClearLedger Infrastructure Contract\n\nProvision the ClearLedger clearing and settlement platform using Terraform or OpenTofu in `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state stored at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n\n## Required Input Source\n\nRead runtime inputs from `/workspace/config/config.json`:\n\n- `resource_prefix`: unique prefix for this deployment (for example `cl-a1b2c3`)\n- `region`: AWS region (`us-east-1`)\n- `aws_endpoint_url`: AWS control-plane endpoint (`http://aws:4566`)\n- `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`: PostgreSQL database name and credentials\n- `api_image`, `projector_image`, `relay_image`, `archiver_image`: container image references\n- `api_image_id`, `projector_image_id`, `relay_image_id`, `archiver_image_id`: image digest identifiers\n\nEvery taggable AWS resource created by your configuration must carry the tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` (at least 25 tagged resources) and have its name or identifier prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`. All cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared in Terraform/OpenTofu (`aws_vpc`, `aws_subnet`, `aws_internet_gateway`, `aws_route_table`, `aws_security_group`, `aws_lb`, `aws_lb_target_group`, `aws_lb_listener`, `aws_ecs_cluster`, `aws_ecs_task_definition`, `aws_ecs_service`, `aws_db_subnet_group`, `aws_db_instance`, `aws_dynamodb_table`, `aws_elasticache_subnet_group`, `aws_elasticache_replication_group`, `aws_s3_bucket`, `aws_s3_bucket_versioning`, `aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`, `aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`, `aws_sqs_queue`, `aws_lambda_function`, `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping`, `aws_scheduler_schedule`, `aws_cognito_user_pool`, `aws_cognito_resource_server`, `aws_cognito_user_pool_client`, `aws_iam_role`, `aws_iam_role_policy`, `aws_kms_key`, `aws_kms_alias`, `aws_cloudwatch_log_group`) rather than created via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`.\n\n## Service Specifications\n\nRead the individual service contracts in `/workspace/contracts/services/` for the exact resource attributes and wiring requirements:\n\n- [VPC and Security Groups](services/vpc.md)\n- [Application Load Balancer](services/alb.md)\n- [ECS Fargate Service](services/ecs.md)\n- [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md)\n- [SQS Main Queue and DLQ](services/sqs.md)\n- [Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping](services/lambda.md)\n- [DynamoDB Projection Store](services/dynamodb.md)\n- [ElastiCache for Valkey](services/elasticache-valkey.md)\n- [S3 Audit Archive](services/s3.md)\n- [EventBridge Scheduler](services/scheduler.md)\n- [Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes](services/cognito.md)\n- [IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies](services/iam.md)\n- [Customer-Managed KMS Keys](services/kms.md)\n- [CloudWatch Log Groups](services/cloudwatch-logs.md)\n\n## Submission Scripts and Manifest\n\n1. `/workspace/submission/deploy.sh`:\n   - Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store & Control-Plane Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane (security groups, IAM role policies, SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge schedules, CloudWatch Log Groups, Lambda environment variables, and PostgreSQL schema/constraints/triggers/indexes) and all derived data stores (SQS outbox relay, DynamoDB projections, Valkey cache entries, and the versioned S3 `ledger-audit/` archive) against PostgreSQL as specified in `services/rds.md`, `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, `services/s3.md`, `services/iam.md`, and `services/vpc.md` before exiting `0`.\n- **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n  - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including customer-managed KMS keys scheduled for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n- **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n\n\n# ClearLedger Infrastructure Contract\n\nProvision the ClearLedger clearing and settlement platform using Terraform or OpenTofu in `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state stored at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n\n## Required Input Source\n\nRead runtime inputs from `/workspace/config/config.json`:\n\n- `resource_prefix`: unique prefix for this deployment (for example `cl-a1b2c3`)\n- `region`: AWS region (`us-east-1`)\n- `aws_endpoint_url`: AWS control-plane endpoint (`http://aws:4566`)\n- `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`: PostgreSQL database name and credentials\n- `api_image`, `projector_image`, `relay_image`, `archiver_image`: container image references\n- `api_image_id`, `projector_image_id`, `relay_image_id`, `archiver_image_id`: image digest identifiers\n\nEvery taggable AWS resource created by your configuration must carry the tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` (at least 25 tagged resources) and have its name or identifier prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`. All cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared in Terraform/OpenTofu (`aws_vpc`, `aws_subnet`, `aws_internet_gateway`, `aws_route_table`, `aws_security_group`, `aws_lb`, `aws_lb_target_group`, `aws_lb_listener`, `aws_ecs_cluster`, `aws_ecs_task_definition`, `aws_ecs_service`, `aws_db_subnet_group`, `aws_db_instance`, `aws_dynamodb_table`, `aws_elasticache_subnet_group`, `aws_elasticache_replication_group`, `aws_s3_bucket`, `aws_s3_bucket_versioning`, `aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`, `aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`, `aws_sqs_queue`, `aws_lambda_function`, `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping`, `aws_scheduler_schedule`, `aws_cognito_user_pool`, `aws_cognito_resource_server`, `aws_cognito_user_pool_client`, `aws_iam_role`, `aws_iam_role_policy`, `aws_kms_key`, `aws_kms_alias`, `aws_cloudwatch_log_group`) rather than created via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`.\n\n## Service Specifications\n\nRead the individual service contracts in `/workspace/contracts/services/` for the exact resource attributes and wiring requirements:\n\n- [VPC and Security Groups](services/vpc.md)\n- [Application Load Balancer](services/alb.md)\n- [ECS Fargate Service](services/ecs.md)\n- [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md)\n- [SQS Main Queue and DLQ](services/sqs.md)\n- [Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping](services/lambda.md)\n- [DynamoDB Projection Store](services/dynamodb.md)\n- [ElastiCache for Valkey](services/elasticache-valkey.md)\n- [S3 Audit Archive](services/s3.md)\n- [EventBridge Scheduler](services/scheduler.md)\n- [Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes](services/cognito.md)\n- [IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies](services/iam.md)\n- [Customer-Managed KMS Keys](services/kms.md)\n- [CloudWatch Log Groups](services/cloudwatch-logs.md)\n\n## Submission Scripts and Manifest\n\n1. `/workspace/submission/deploy.sh`:\n   - Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE`/`BEFORE DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Canonical trimmed strings and length bounds: `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, and `current_stage` (plus `last_memo` when non-null) must be stored in canonical trimmed form (`col = btrim(col)`, no leading or trailing whitespace) and match the length bounds and allowed status values of `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with `debit_party <> credit_party` and `version >= 1`.\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `current_stage = 'INITIATED@' || debit_party`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, `last_memo = 'Settlement initiated'`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`, and `updated_at > created_at`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`) and a strictly increasing `updated_at` timestamp (`NEW.updated_at > OLD.updated_at`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n  - `clearledger.settlements` is an append-only ledger aggregate root (`DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with canonical trimmed storage (`col = btrim(col)`) and length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null canonically trimmed `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` canonically trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` with `data.clearingStage = 'INITIATED@' || (data.debitParty)` and `data.memo = 'Settlement initiated'` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be strictly greater (`>`) than the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`, with canonical trimmed `correlation_id` (`correlation_id = btrim(correlation_id)`).\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `attempts = 0`, both `published_at` and `last_error` must be `NULL`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; when `last_error IS NOT NULL`, `published_at IS NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `length(btrim(last_error)) > 0`, and `last_error = btrim(last_error)`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), canonically trimmed `idempotency_key` (`idempotency_key = btrim(idempotency_key)` and `8..128` chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`, and each `response_body->>'eventId'` (as well as each `(response_body->>'settlementId', (response_body->>'version')::integer)` pair) must be unique across `clearledger.idempotency_keys`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n=== sqs\n# SQS Main Queue and Dead-Letter Queue (`services/sqs.md`)\n\n- Provision a dead-letter queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) encrypted with the customer-managed `messaging` KMS key (`kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`) and `message_retention_seconds = 1209600` (14 days).\n- Provision the main event queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) with:\n  - `alb`:\n    - **Ingress**: TCP port `80` (`from_port = 80`, `to_port = 80`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from `0.0.0.0/0`.\n    - **Egress**: TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) targeting the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (must not have explicit TCP/UDP egress rules allowing `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`).\n  - `ecs`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `alb` security group ID (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or any CIDR block).\n    - **Egress**: outbound traffic to the AWS control plane and data stores.\n  - `rds`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `5432` (`from_port = 5432`, `to_port = 5432`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`).\n    - **Egress**: no explicit outbound TCP/UDP egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections).\n  - `valkey`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `6379` (`from_port = 6379`, `to_port = 6379`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`).\n    - **Egress**: no explicit outbound TCP/UDP egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections).\n- During `deploy.sh` convergence, revoke any out-of-band public (`0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`) ingress rules added to `ecs`, `rds`, or `valkey` and any out-of-band egress rules added to `rds` or `valkey`.\n- Tag the VPC, subnets, internet gateway, route tables, and security groups with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`.\n\n\n=== alb\n# Application Load Balancer (`services/alb.md`)\n\n- Provision an internet-facing Application Load Balancer (`aws_lb`, `load_balancer_type = \"application\"`, `internal = false`) attached to the two public subnets and the `alb` security group.\n- Provision an HTTP target group (`aws_lb_target_group`) on port `8080` (`protocol = \"HTTP\"`, `target_type = \"ip\"`) with a health check configured for:\n  - `engine = \"postgres\"`\n  - `engine_version = \"16.3\"` (or `16.*`)\n  - `instance_class = \"db.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `db_name`, `username`, and `password` from `/workspace/config/config.json`\n  - `storage_encrypted = true`\n  - `kms_key_id` set to the customer-managed `database` KMS key ARN (`kms.database_arn`)\n  - `publicly_accessible = false`\n  - `skip_final_snapshot = true`\n  - `vpc_security_group_ids` containing the `rds` security group\n- Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data.\n\n## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n\nThe pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, all three indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n\n`deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all three indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance, and ensure that all user-defined triggers on all four tables are enabled (`tgenabled = 'O'`, re-enabling any trigger disabled out-of-band via `ALTER TABLE ... ENABLE TRIGGER ALL`).\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`, with canonical trimmed `correlation_id` (`correlation_id = btrim(correlation_id)`).\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `attempts = 0`, both `published_at` and `last_error` must be `NULL`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; when `last_error IS NOT NULL`, `published_at IS NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `length(btrim(last_error)) > 0`, and `last_error = btrim(last_error)`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), canonically trimmed `idempotency_key` (`idempotency_key = btrim(idempotency_key)` and `8..128` chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`, and each `response_body->>'eventId'` (as well as each `(response_body->>'settlementId', (response_body->>'version')::integer)` pair) must be unique across `clearledger.idempotency_keys`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n=== sqs\n# SQS Main Queue and Dead-Letter Queue (`services/sqs.md`)\n\n- Provision a dead-letter queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) encrypted with the customer-managed `messaging` KMS key (`kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`) and `message_retention_seconds = 1209600` (14 days).\n- Provision the main event queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) with:\n  - ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, batch `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals are strictly disjoint and non-overlapping across objects, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, contiguous gap-free iam\n# IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Tag each of the six IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` and `ClearLedgerRole = <ecs_execution|ecs_task|projector|relay|archiver|scheduler>`.\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys, and every role must explicitly deny (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four customer-managed KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`).\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four KMS keys as well as all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies or attached managed policies added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy, and any unattached customer-managed IAM policies prefixed with `<resource_prefix>` must also be deleted.\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Read-only access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:Query`, `dynamodb:DescribeTable`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, consuming or deleting SQS messages (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; mutating DynamoDB (`dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-API log group (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`). sql IMMUTABLE AS $f$\n  SELECT COALESCE(t IN ('INITIATED','VALIDATED','RESERVED','CLEARED','SETTLED','RECONCILED','DISPUTED'), false)\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_rank(s 4 WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5 END\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_transition_ok(old_s text, new_s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $f$\n  SELECT COALESCE(CASE\n    WHEN old_s = 'RECONCILED' THEN false\n    WHEN old_s = 'DISPUTED' THEN new_s IN ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n    WHEN clearledger.status_rank(old_s) IS NULL THEN false\n    WHEN new_s = 'DISPUTED' THEN true\n    WHEN clearledger.status_rank(new_s) IS NULL THEN false\n    ELSE clearledger.status_rank(new_s) >= clearledger.status_rank(old_s)\n  END, false)\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.safe_ts(t text) RETURNS timestamptz\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS $f$\nBEGIN\n  IF t IS NULL OR t !~ '^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}[Tt]\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2}(\\.\\d+)?([Zz]|[+-]\\d{2}:\\d{2})$' THEN\n    RETURN NULL;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN t::timestamptz;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN NULL;\nEND\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.safe_uuid(t text) RETURNS uuid\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $f$\n  SELECT CASE WHEN clearledger.is_uuid(t) THEN t::uuid END\n$f$;\n\n-- Strict structural validation of ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (events.schema.json).\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.envelope_ok(p jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS $f$\nDECLARE\n  k text; d jsonb; v integer; et text; kind text; st text; stage text; debit text;\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(p) LOOP\n    IF k <> ALL (ARRAY['schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId',\n                       'aggregateVersion','occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data']) THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (p ?& ARRAY['schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId',\n                     'aggregateVersion','occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data']) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'schemaVersion') <> 'string' OR p->>'schemaVersion' <> '1.0' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'eventId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid(p->>'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid(p->>'aggregateId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'eventType') <> 'string' OR p->>'eventType' NOT IN ('SettlementInitiated','LedgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateType') <> 'string' OR p->>'aggregateType' <> 'settlement' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateVersion') <> 'number' OR (p->>'aggregateVersion') !~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'occurredAt') <> 'string' OR clearledger.safe_ts(p->>'occurredAt') IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'correlationId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.str_ok(p->>'correlationId', 4, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'idempotencyKey') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.str_ok(p->>'idempotencyKey', 8, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  v := (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer;\n  et := p->>'eventType';\n  d := p->'data';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(d) LOOP\n    IF k <> ALL (ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','entryId','status','clearingStage','memo']) THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (d ?& ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','status','clearingStage']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT unnest(ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','status','clearingStage']) LOOP\n    IF jsonb_typeof(d->k) <> 'string' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  kind := d->>'kind'; st := d->>'status'; stage := d->>'clearingStage'; debit := d->>'debitParty';\n  IF kind NOT IN ('settlementInitiated','ledgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.str_ok(d->>'accountId', 3, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.str_ok(d->>'reference', 3, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.str_ok(debit, 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.str_ok(d->>'creditParty', 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF debit = d->>'creditParty' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_status(st) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'memo' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') <> 'null' THEN\n    IF jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.str_ok(d->>'memo', 1, 256) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END IF;\n  IF d ? 'entryId' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') <> 'null' THEN\n    IF jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid(d->>'entryId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF v = 1 THEN\n    IF et <> 'SettlementInitiated' OR kind <> 'settlementInitiated' OR st <> 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF d->>'entryId' IS NOT NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF stage <> 'INITIATED@' || debit THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF d->>'memo' IS DISTINCT FROM 'Settlement initiated' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF et <> 'LedgerEntryRecorded' OR kind <> 'ledgerEntryRecorded' OR st = 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF d->>'entryId' IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.str_ok(stage, 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN true;\nEND\n$f$;\n\n-- Column <-> envelope equality (shared by events and outbox).\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.envelope_matches(\n  p jsonb, c_event_id uuid, c_settlement_id uuid, c_version integer, c_correlation text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE AS $f$\n  SELECT COALESCE(\n    clearledger.safe_uuid(p->>'eventId') = c_event_id\n    AND clearledger.safe_uuid(p->>'aggregateId') = c_settlement_id\n    AND CASE WHEN (p->>'aggregateVersion') ~ '^[0-9]{1,9}$' THEN (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer END = c_version\n    AND p->>'correlationId' = c_correlation, false)\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.event_matches(\n  p jsonb, c_event_id uuid, c_settlement_id uuid, c_version integer, c_type text,\n  c_correlation text, c_idem text, c_occurred timestamptz) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE AS $f$\n  SELECT COALESCE(\n    clearledger.envelope_matches(p, c_event_id, c_settlement_id, c_version, c_correlation)\n    AND p->>'eventType' = c_type\n    AND p->>'idempotencyKey' = c_idem\n    AND clearledger.safe_ts(p->>'occurredAt') = c_occurred, false)\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.write_response_ok(b jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $f$\nDECLARE k text;\nBEGIN\n  IF b IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(b) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(b) LOOP\n    IF k <> ALL (ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (b ?& ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  RETURN COALESCE(\n    jsonb_typeof(b->'settlementId') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid(b->>'settlementId')\n    AND jsonb_typeof(b->'eventId') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid(b->>'eventId')\n    AND jsonb_typeof(b->'version') = 'number' AND (b->>'version') ~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$'\n    AND b->'accepted' = 'true'::jsonb\n    AND b->'idempotentReplay' = 'false'::jsonb, false);\nEND\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idem_row_ok(scope text, code integer, b jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $f$\nDECLARE ver integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF scope IS NULL OR code IS NULL OR NOT clearledger.write_response_ok(b) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  ver := (b->>'version')::integer;\n  IF scope ~* '^create:[0-9a-f-]{36}$' THEN\n    RETURN lower(substr(scope, 8)) = lower(b->>'settlementId') AND code = 201 AND ver = 1;\n  ELSIF scope ~* '^entry:[0-9a-f-]{36}$' THEN\n    RETURN lower(substr(scope, 7)) = lower(b->>'settlementId') AND code = 202 AND ver >= 2;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$f$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- tables\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.settlements (\n  settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n  account_id    TEXT NOT NULL,\n  reference     TEXT NOT NULL,\n  debit_party   TEXT NOT NULL,\n  credit_party  TEXT NOT NULL,\n  current_status TEXT NOT NULL,\n  current_stage  TEXT NOT NULL,\n  last_entry_id UUID NULL,\n  last_memo     TEXT NULL,\n  version       INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  entry_count   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.events (\n  seq            BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n  event_id       UUID NOT NULL,\n  settlement_id  UUID NOT NULL,\n  aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  event_type     TEXT NOT NULL,\n  correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,\n  occurred_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n  payload        JSONB NOT NULL,\n  created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.outbox (\n  seq            BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n  event_id       UUID NOT NULL,\n  settlement_id  UUID NOT NULL,\n  aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  payload        JSONB NOT NULL,\n  created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  published_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n  archived_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n  attempts       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  last_error     TEXT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.idempotency_keys (\n  scope           TEXT NOT NULL,\n  idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,\n  request_hash    TEXT NOT NULL,\n  status_code     INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  response_body   JSONB NOT NULL,\n  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)\n);\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- constraints (added only when missing)\nCREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.add_con(tbl regclass, cname text, def text) RETURNS void\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conrelid = tbl AND conname = cname) THEN\n    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s ADD CONSTRAINT %I %s', tbl, cname, def);\n  END IF;\nEND\n$f$;\n\n-- settlements\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.settlements', 'ck_settlements_canonical', $d$CHECK (\n  clearledger.str_ok(account_id, 3, 64) AND clearledger.str_ok(reference, 3, 64)\n  AND clearledger.str_ok(debit_party, 2, 64) AND clearledger.str_ok(credit_party, 2, 64)\n  AND clearledger.is_canonical(current_stage)\n  AND (last_memo IS NULL OR clearledger.str_ok(last_memo, 1, 256))\n  AND debit_party <> credit_party)$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.settlements', 'ck_settlements_status', $d$CHECK (clearledger.is_status(current_status))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.settlements', 'ck_settlements_version', $d$CHECK (version >= 1 AND entry_count >= 0 AND entry_count = version - 1)$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.settlements', 'ck_settlements_lifecycle', $d$CHECK (\n  CASE WHEN version = 1 THEN\n         entry_count = 0 AND current_status = 'INITIATED'\n         AND current_stage = 'INITIATED@' || debit_party\n         AND last_entry_id IS NULL AND last_memo = 'Settlement initiated'\n         AND updated_at = created_at\n       ELSE\n         entry_count = version - 1 AND current_status <> 'INITIATED'\n         AND char_length(current_stage) BETWEEN 2 AND 64\n         AND last_entry_id IS NOT NULL AND updated_at > created_at\n  END)$d$);\n\n-- events\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.events', 'uq_events_event_id', 'UNIQUE (event_id)');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.events', 'uq_events_settlement_version', 'UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.events', 'uq_events_settlement_idempotency', 'UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.events', 'fk_events_settlement',\n  'FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id) REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.events', 'ck_events_columns', $d$CHECK (\n  aggregate_version >= 1 AND event_type IN ('SettlementInitiated', 'LedgerEntryRecorded')\n  AND clearledger.str_ok(correlation_id, 4, 128) AND clearledger.str_ok(idempotency_key, 8, 128))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.events', 'ck_events_envelope', $d$CHECK (clearledger.envelope_ok(payload))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.events', 'ck_events_envelope_columns', $d$CHECK (\n  clearledger.event_matches(payload, event_id, settlement_id, aggregate_version, event_type,\n                            correlation_id, idempotency_key, occurred_at))$d$);\n\n-- outbox\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'uq_outbox_event_id', 'UNIQUE (event_id)');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'uq_outbox_settlement_version', 'UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'fk_outbox_event',\n  'FOREIGN KEY (event_id) REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'fk_outbox_settlement',\n  'FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id) REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'fk_outbox_event_version',\n  'FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'ck_outbox_columns', $d$CHECK (\n  aggregate_version >= 1 AND clearledger.str_ok(correlation_id, 4, 128))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'ck_outbox_envelope', $d$CHECK (\n  clearledger.envelope_ok(payload)\n  AND clearledger.envelope_matches(payload, event_id, settlement_id, aggregate_version, correlation_id))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'ck_outbox_attempts', $d$CHECK (\n  attempts >= 0 AND (attempts > 0 OR (published_at IS NULL AND last_error IS NULL)))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'ck_outbox_published', $d$CHECK (\n  published_at IS NULL OR (attempts >= 1 AND last_error IS NULL AND published_at >= created_at))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'ck_outbox_last_error', $d$CHECK (\n  last_error IS NULL OR (published_at IS NULL AND attempts >= 1\n                         AND length(btrim(last_error)) > 0 AND last_error = btrim(last_error)))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.outbox', 'ck_outbox_archived', $d$CHECK (\n  archived_at IS NULL OR (published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at >= published_at))$d$);\n\n-- idempotency_keys\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'ck_idem_scope', $d$CHECK (\n  scope ~* '^(create|entry):[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$')$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'ck_idem_key', $d$CHECK (clearledger.str_ok(idempotency_key, 8, 128))$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'ck_idem_hash', $d$CHECK (request_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$')$d$);\nSELECT pg_temp.add_con('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'ck_idem_response', $d$CHECK (\n  clearledger.idem_row_ok(scope, status_code, response_body))$d$);\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- trigger functions\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_settlements_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.settlements is append-only: DELETE rejected';\n  ELSIF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN\n    IF NEW.version <> 1 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % must be created at version 1 (got %)', NEW.settlement_id, NEW.version;\n    END IF;\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n  -- UPDATE\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED and terminal', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id OR NEW.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reference OR NEW.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.credit_party OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % header columns are immutable', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version <> OLD.version + 1 OR NEW.entry_count <> OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % must advance version and entry_count by exactly 1', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NULL OR NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % update requires a new last_entry_id', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at <= OLD.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must strictly increase', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % illegal status transition % -> %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_events_insert_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  d jsonb := NEW.payload->'data';\n  cnt bigint; mx integer;\n  prev_at timestamptz; prev_status text;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % references unknown settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT count(*), COALESCE(max(aggregate_version), 0) INTO cnt, mx\n    FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> mx + 1 OR cnt <> mx THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event version % for settlement % is not contiguous (latest %)', NEW.aggregate_version, NEW.settlement_id, mx;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF d->>'entryId' IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n        WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n          AND e.payload->'data'->>'entryId' = d->>'entryId') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', d->>'entryId', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF s.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'accountId' OR s.reference IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'reference'\n     OR s.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'debitParty' OR s.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'creditParty'\n     OR s.current_status IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'status' OR s.current_stage IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'clearingStage'\n     OR s.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM clearledger.safe_uuid(d->>'entryId')\n     OR s.last_memo IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'memo' OR s.version <> NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR s.updated_at <> NEW.occurred_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % does not match settlement % state', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 THEN\n    IF s.created_at <> NEW.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'initial event % must occur at settlement creation time', NEW.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    SELECT e.occurred_at, e.payload->'data'->>'status' INTO prev_at, prev_status\n      FROM clearledger.events e\n     WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND e.aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF prev_at IS NULL OR NEW.occurred_at <= prev_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % occurred_at must be after the preceding event', NEW.event_id;\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(prev_status, d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % illegal status transition % -> %', NEW.event_id, prev_status, d->>'status';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_append_only() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION '%.% is append-only: % rejected', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_OP;\nEND\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_insert_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nDECLARE\n  e clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  cnt bigint; mx integer;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO e FROM clearledger.events WHERE event_id = NEW.event_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row references unknown event %', NEW.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF e.settlement_id <> NEW.settlement_id OR e.aggregate_version <> NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR e.correlation_id <> NEW.correlation_id OR e.payload IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.payload THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row for event % does not mirror the event', NEW.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  SELECT count(*), COALESCE(max(aggregate_version), 0) INTO cnt, mx\n    FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> mx + 1 OR cnt <> mx THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox version % for settlement % is not contiguous (latest %)', NEW.aggregate_version, NEW.settlement_id, mx;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_change_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.seq <> OLD.seq OR NEW.event_id <> OLD.event_id OR NEW.settlement_id <> OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version <> OLD.aggregate_version OR NEW.correlation_id <> OLD.correlation_id\n     OR NEW.payload IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.payload OR NEW.created_at <> OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns of event % are immutable', OLD.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts of event % cannot decrease', OLD.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    IF NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL AND (NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts OR NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL) THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing outbox event % requires attempts to increment and archived_at to be NULL', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay: reset published_at and archived_at together\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL OR NEW.attempts <> OLD.attempts OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox replay reset of event % may only clear published_at and archived_at', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF NEW.published_at <> OLD.published_at OR NEW.attempts <> OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published outbox event % cannot change published_at, attempts or last_error', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at <> OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at of outbox event % cannot change without resetting it to NULL first', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$f$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_idem_insert_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nDECLARE\n  b jsonb := NEW.response_body;\n  eid uuid := clearledger.safe_uuid(b->>'eventId');\n  sid uuid := clearledger.safe_uuid(b->>'settlementId');\n  ver integer := (b->>'version')::integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n                  WHERE e.event_id = eid AND e.settlement_id = sid AND e.aggregate_version = ver\n                    AND e.idempotency_key = NEW.idempotency_key) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency record references no matching event %', eid;\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.outbox o\n                  WHERE o.event_id = eid AND o.settlement_id = sid AND o.aggregate_version = ver) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency record references no matching outbox row for event %', eid;\n  END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.idempotency_keys k\n              WHERE k.response_body->>'eventId' = b->>'eventId'\n                 OR (k.response_body->>'settlementId' = b->>'settlementId'\n                     AND (k.response_body->>'version')::integer = ver)) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % is already bound to another idempotency record', eid;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$f$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- triggers\nCREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.add_trg(tbl regclass, tname text, def text) RETURNS void\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $f$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger WHERE tgrelid = tbl AND tgname = tname AND NOT tgisinternal) THEN\n    EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I %s', tname, def);\n  END IF;\nEND\n$f$;\n\nSELECT pg_temp.add_trg('clearledger.settlements', 'trg_settlements_guard',\n  'BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.settlements FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_settlements_guard()');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_trg('clearledger.events', 'trg_events_insert_guard',\n  'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.events FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_events_insert_guard()');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_trg('clearledger.events', 'trg_events_append_only',\n  'BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.events FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_append_only()');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_trg('clearledger.outbox', 'trg_outbox_insert_guard',\n  'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_insert_guard()');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_trg('clearledger.outbox', 'trg_outbox_change_guard',\n  'BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_change_guard()');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_trg('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'trg_idem_insert_guard',\n  'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.idempotency_keys FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_idem_insert_guard()');\nSELECT pg_temp.add_trg('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'trg_idem_append_only',\n  'BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.idempotency_keys FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_append_only()');\n\n-- Re-enable anything disabled out-of-band (user triggers only; system FK triggers need superuser).\nALTER TABLE clearledger.settlements ENABLE TRIGGER USER;\nALTER TABLE clearledger.events ENABLE TRIGGER USER;\nALTER TABLE clearledger.outbox ENABLE TRIGGER USER;\nALTER TABLE clearledger.idempotency_keys ENABLE TRIGGER USER;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- indexes\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished\n  ON clearledger.outbox (seq) WHERE published_at IS NULL;\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived\n  ON clearledger.outbox (seq) WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL;\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version\n  ON clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version);\n\nCOMMIT;\nEOF\ncd /workspace/submission; export PGPASSWORD='Cl1eb8ee2027!'; psql -h aws -p 7001
